avaḷatu tōṭṭattil aṉṟu tōṭṭakkāraṉuṭaṉ avaṉ naṇpar cilar pēcikkoṇṭiruntaṉar. tōṭṭattiṉ nērttikaṇṭu naṇparkaḷ tōṭṭakkāraṉ tiṟattai mecciṉar. appōtu avaṉ makiḻvataṟku māṟākak kōṇiya mukattuṭaṉ, “aṉparīr, ittōṭṭattai nīṅkaḷ meccuvataṟku māṟākap paḻittirukka vēṇṭum. itaṉ nērtti koṇṭu eṉṉa payaṉ. iṉṟaiya nam iṅkilāntu itaṉiṉum periyatoru tōṭṭam eṉalām. atuvaṉṟō nērttiyāyirukka vēṇṭum? āṉāl aḵtu aṅṅaṉam illaiyē! ataṉ tōṭṭakkāraṉ nallavaṉum naṉmuyaṟci uṭaiyavaṉuntāṉ. āṉāl, pūvai vaḷarttup puḻuvaiyum vaḷarttuviṭṭatu pōl nāṭṭumakkaḷiṭam kāṭṭum atē aṉpai avarkaḷ uyirai uṟiñcum camayacañcīvikaḷiṭamum kāṭṭuvatāl nāṭṭai avaṉ avarkaḷukku iraiyākki viṭṭāṉ. mēlum. perum makkaḷaik kaḷaikaḷ pōl vaḷaraviṭṭup potumakkaḷākiya payirkaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyait taṭaippaṭuttiyum vantirukkāṉ. taṉ kavaṉakkuṟaivāl nērnta tīṅkukaḷaip pāvam avaṉ aṟiyāṉ! ittōṭṭattai mecca nērntiruppiṉ ettaṉaiyō nalamāyiruntirukkum” eṉṟu kūṟip perumūccu viṭṭāṉ.
tōṭṭattaik kākkum muṟaiyil taṉakkirukkum tiṟaṉ iṅkilāntaik kākkum muṟaiyil riccarṭukku illai eṉṟu at tōṭṭakkāraṉ kūṟiya kuṟippaik kēṭṭu mutalil kaṉaṉṟeḻunta araci, kaṇavaṉukku ētō tīṅkeṉa uḷḷūrak kaṇṭataṉ payaṉākat tōṭṭak kāraṉai aṇuki, “nēyaṉē, aracaṉ ayalnāṭu ceṉṟu pala nāḷākiyum varakkāṇēṉ; kavalaippaṭukiṟēṉ; avaṉ araciyalukku ūṟu nērntateṉak kaṇṭāl eṉakkuk kūṟuvāy” eṉṟāḷ.
tōṭṭakkāraṉ taṉ puṉmoḻikaḷ iṅṅaṉam etirpārāta periya iṭattaic ceṉṟu cērntatē eṉa varuttamum veṭkamum aṭaintāṉ. piṉ araciyiṉ mukanōkki āṇṭu araciyal perumaikku māṟākap peṇmaiyiṉ melliyalpum kavalaiyum kuṭikoṇṭirukkak kaṇṭu parivuṟṟu, heṉṟi heripōrṭu paṭaiyuṭaṉ vantiruppatai nārtamparlantu mutaliyōr etiripakkam cērntiruppataiyum viḷaṅkak kūṟiṉāṉ. kēṭṭa araci tīkkāṟṟu vīcap peṟṟa māntaḷireṉac cāmpat talaippaṭṭāḷ.
3. alaimēl alai
ayarlāntilirukkum riccarṭukku icceytikaḷiṉ muḻu vivaramum eṭṭavillaiyāyiṉum nilaimai nerukkaṭiyāṉatu eṉṟa aḷavilēṉum ceytikaḷ vantu koṇṭiruntaṉa. kāṟṟu toṭarcciyākap pala nāṭkaḷ iṅkilāntiṉiṉṟu ayarlāntu nōkki aṭittatiṉāl, icceytikaḷ iṅkilāntiṉiṉṟu ayarlāntu vantu cērntum riccarṭu ayarlāntiliruntu iṅkilāntu varamuṭiyāmaliruntatu. ikkāla tāmatattāl ovvoru noṭiyum heṉṟiyiṉ valimai vaḷarcciyaṭaintatum riccarṭiṉ valimai kuṉṟiyum vantaṉa. riccarṭu iṅkilāntil vantiṟaṅkiyapōtu, tīcceytikaḷ pala avaṉ kātil vantuviḻak kāttiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmaiyāṉatu vēlsil sālispari talaimaiyiluḷḷa paṭaikaḷ kalaintu pōyiṉa eṉpatē. avaṟṟiṭaiyē riccarṭu iṟantu pōṉāṉ eṉṟa poyc ceyti parantatē avai kalaintu pōṉataṉ kāraṇam eṉṟu teriyavantatu. taṉ tāmatattāl nērnta ipperun tīṅkaik kēḷviyuṟṟu riccarṭu poṟi kalaṅki niṉṟāṉ. caṟṟuk kaḻintapiṉ, avaṉ taṉ pakkam niṉṟa  yārkkōmakaṉ makaṉākiya 16āmerla perumakaṉai nōkkic caṟṟu maṉattērtaluṭaṉ, “āyiṉum eṉṉa, kēṭoṉṟum illai; niṉ tantaiyiṭam iṉṉum nam nāṭṭup paṭaiyiṉ oru pakuti uṇṭaṉṟō? ataṉ valiyē namakkup pōtum” eṉṟāṉ.
avvamayam tūtaṉ oruvaṉ vantu, yārk kōmakaṉ pārklik kōṭṭaiyiṉuḷ heṉṟiyai varaviṭṭataiyum, akkōṭṭaiyum atiluḷḷa yārk kōmakaṉ paṭaikaḷam etiri pakkam cērntataiyum kūṟiṉāṉ. ataṉaiyaṭuttāṟ pōl iṉṉoruvaṉ vantu, vorsṭar kōmakaṉ etiriyiṭam mītip paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟataiyum nārtamparlantu mutaliyōr etirikkut tuṇaiyāka varuvataiyum kūṟiṉāṉ.
alaimēl alai aṭittatupōl vanta icceyti kaḷāl mōtuṇṭu caṟṟē amiḻntu niṉṟa riccarṭiṉ uḷḷamākiya kaṭṭumaram, ataṉ iyaṟkai noymmaiyāl mīṇṭum mīṇṭum mēleḻuntu kaṭalmītu pāyntu cellavē toṭaṅkiṟṟu. naṉmaiyē niṟainta veṟṟi nāṭkaḷil cerukkāyt tōṉṟiya avaṉatu taṟperumai, tōlviyiṉ niḻal kaṇmuṉ āṭum innāṭkaḷil vīramamākavum perumitamākavum māṟi avaṉuṭaiya uṭaleḻilukku ōr aracurimaip perumai nalkiyatu. taṉṉai aṭuttōrai iccamayam avaṉ, taṉ vaṉappālum taṉṉaic cūḻnta   tuyarattiṉ kuṟikaḷālum taṉ kavittiṟa mikka moḻic cittiraṅkaḷālum taṉ pakkamāka īrttu, avarkaḷmītu putuvakaiyāṉatōr āṭci celuttiṉāṉ.
‘cāvap pōkiṟavaṉ turumpaiyum puṇaiyākak koḷvāṉ’ eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. āṉāl, attakaiyōr atturumpaip puṇaiyāka nampuvatumillai; nampuvatākak kāṭṭuvatumillai. āṉāl, kuḻantai pōl cūtaṟṟa taṉmaiyuṭaiya riccarṭukku, iccamayam turumpukūṭa uṇmaiyil puṇaiyākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu. oruvēḷai tuyarnīkka atu puṇaiyākat tōṉṟiyirā viṭṭāluṅkūṭa attuyariṭaiyē tāṉ ciṟumaippaṭātu kākkum aḷavukkēṉum avaṉ attakaiya turumpaip puṇaiyeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉṉal vēṇṭum. ēṉeṉil, nampikkaiyiḻanta an nilaiyilum camaya uṇmai kaṇṭa antaṇāḷaṉākiya 17kārlail talaimakaṉ avaṉai nōkki, “aracē, maṉitar tuṉai akaṉṟateṉak koṇṭu kavalaṟka; iṟaivaṉatu tuṇaiyiṉ niṟaivu tamakku uḷatu. umakku aracurimai tantatu maṉitarallavē! av aracurimai tanta atē iṟai iṉṟu ataṉ pakaivarai aḻittu umakku atu nilaikkumāṟum ceyyum” eṉṟatē, riccarṭu taṉ nilaimaṟantu avaṉ āṟutalē oru tēṟutalākak koṇṭu, “ām aiyaṉē, eṉakkup paṟṟukkōṭāyuḷḷa appēruṇmaiyai maṟantēṉ. nīr naṉku niṉaivūṭṭiṉīr. iṉik kavalaippaṭak kāraṇamillai” eṉṟu nakaittāṉ. ulakiṉ mōcaṭi naṭaikaḷai uṇarnta avaṉ veḷḷaiyuḷḷam kaṇṭu avaṉ naṇparum piṟarum parivukoṇṭu varuntiṉar.
heṉṟiyiṉ paṭaivali mēṉmēlumperukik koṇṭē vantatu. riccarṭiṉmītu poṟāmai koṇṭa perumakkaḷ. avaṉatu kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa iṉpa vāḻkkaiyai veṟutta maṭattut tuṟaviyar, avaṉ naṇparkaḷāṉa camayacañcīvikaḷaiyum avaṉaiyum veṟutta piṟamakkaḷ mutaliya palarum laṇṭaṉ nōkki varum heṉṟiyiṉ paṭaiyil vantu cērntaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam paṭaivali mikutiyāka mikutiyāka heṉṟiyiṉ taṟperumaiyum perunōkkum kūṭavē vaḷarntu koṇṭu vantaṉa. avaṉ aracaṉ eṉṟa peyar illāmalēyē aracaṉurimaikaḷai ovvoṉṟāyp mēṟkoḷḷalāṉāṉ. mutaṉmutal avaṉ taṉatākkiyatu, tantaiyiṉi ṭamiruntu riccarṭu paṟimutal ceyta pāliṅpurōk perunilak kiḻamaiyākum. uṭaṉa ap perunilakkiḻamaikkuriya paṭṭattuṭaṉ avaṉ heṉṟi pāliṅ purōk eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭāṉ. ituvarai avaṉō ṭotta varāyirunta perumakkaḷ, avaṉ paṭaivaliyum celvākkuṅ kaṇṭu aracar muṉ paṇivatu pōl avaṉ muṉ paṇiyat talaippaṭṭaṉar. avaṉum, aṅṅaṉam paṇiyāta varaiyum riccarṭukku uṭantaiyāṉa varaiyum accuṟuttavum oṟukkavum toṭaṅkiṉāṉ.
ittakaiya peru nilaikaḷuṭaṉ heṉṟi varum vaḻiyil riccarṭiṉ camaya cañcīvikaḷāṉa puṣiyum kirīṉum teṉpaṭa, avaṉ āṭkaḷ avarkaḷaic ciṟaippaṭittu avaṉ muṉṉilaiyiṟ koṇṭu vantu niṟuttiṉar. heṉṟi pāliṅ purōk, avarkaḷai vaḻakku maṉṟattil ucāvuvatu pōl ucāvik kuṟṟañcāṭṭit tāṉē aracaṉeṉṟāṟ pōla avarkaḷait tūkkiliṭumpaṭi uttaraviṭṭāṉ.
pāliṅ purōkkai nōkki vantu koṇṭirunta riccarṭu, taṉ naṇpar ovvoruvarākat taṉṉaiviṭṭu akaṉṟatu kaṇṭu orupuṟam vekuṇṭāṉ; taṉ nilai ōrntu orupuṟam tuyaraṭaintāṉ. maṟṟorupuṟam, iṟaivaṉ kaṭaici noṭiyilēṉum taṉṉāl aracaṉākap paṭaikkappaṭṭa toru pēruyiraik kāppāṉō eṉṟa āval. avaṉ uḷḷattaṭattiṉ iruḷiṭaiyē miṉṉiṟṟu. accamayam tiṭīreṉṟu avaṉakkuk kirīṉ, puṣi mutaliya naṇparkaḷiṉ niṉaivu vantatu. vantatum, avarkaḷēṉum iṉṉum utavuvar eṉṟa nampikkai caṟṟē talaitūkka, avaṉ avarkaḷ  eṅkē  eṉṟu ucāviṉāṉ. arukil iruntavar evarō accamayam, “avarkaḷ appāṟ ceṉṟu cērntaṉarē” eṉṉa, avarkaḷum, taṉṉaik kaiviṭṭu  etiriyiṭam ceṉṟu cērntaṉar eṉṟu tavaṟākak karutat tī mitittavaṉ pōla maṉaṅkotittu, “ā pataṭikaḷ, naṉṟi keṭṭavarkaḷ! avarkaḷum ceṉṟaṉarā? ā, ivvulaka vāḻvaṉaittum ettuṇai veḷippūccu” eṉa araṟṟiṉāṉ. accamayam naṇpar avaṉ koṇṭa tappeṇṇattait tirutti, “aracē! avarkaḷ ummaik kaiviṭṭu appāṟ cellavillai; umakkāka uyirviṭṭu ulakai nīttu appāl emaṉulakukkuc ceṉṟu cōntaṉar” eṉṟaṉar. atu kēṭṭatum riccarṭu ciṉamaṉaittum māṟit taṉ tuyaraiyum maṟantu avarkaḷukkākak kaṇṇīrukuttu maṉa māḻkiṉāṉ.
4. ūḻiṟ peruvali yāvuḷa?
riccarṭu taṉ naṇparuṭaṉ vēls ellaiyiluḷḷa 18piḷiṉṭ kōṭṭaikku vantatum, heṉṟi paṭaiyuṭaṉ aṇmaiyilēyē etirnōkki varukiṟāṉ eṉṟu kēḷvip paṭṭāṉ. avaṉai etirkka vēṇṭivariṉ kōṭṭaiyē pātukāppāṉa iṭam eṉṟeṇṇi riccarṭu akkōṭṭai yilēyē taṅki avaṉai etirpārttiruntāṉ.
ciṉṉērattil heṉṟiṉa pāliṅ purōkkiṉ paṭaiyaic cērnta pērikai oliyum, paṭaivīrar āravāramum piḷiṉṭ kōṭṭaiyarukē vanteṭṭiyatu. kōṭṭai vāyil vantatum heṉṟi nārtamparlantait tūtaṉāka aṉuppiṉāṉ. nārtamparlantu riccarṭu muṉ vantu, “nāṉ heṉṟi heripōrṭu pāliṅ purōk kōmakaṉiṭamiruntu varukiṟēṉ. em talaivar, riccarṭiṉiṭam cila ceytikaḷ pakara vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpukiṟār.”
aracaṉai aracaṉukkuriya muṟaiyilaṉṟi riccarṭu eṉṟumaṭṭuṅ kūṟiyatu kaṇṭu, kāvalarum tuṇaivarum uḷḷūrac cīṟṟaṅ koṇṭaṉar. heṉṟi pāliṅ purōkkiṉ paṭaivaliyum celvākkumē aṉṟu nārtamparlantaik kāttaṉa eṉṉal vēṇṭum. riccarṭu muṉṉilaiyil avaṉ paṇiyātu niṉṟu pēciṉāṉ. ittakaiya naṭaikaḷiliruntum makkaḷ paraparappiliruntum heṉṟiyiṉ uḷḷānt nōkkam taṉakku urimaiyāṉa pāliṅ purōk perunilakkiḻamaiyiṉai maṭṭum peṟuvataṉṟu, ataṉai oru kāraṇamāka vaittuk koṇṭu iṅkilāntiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttu aracurimaiyaik kaippaṟṟuvatēyām eṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
heṉṟiyiṉ celva veṟiyilum celvākkilum mayaṅkip peru makkaḷum naṭunilai makkaḷiṟ palarum, avaṉai aracaṉākka oruppaṭṭuḷḷaṉar eṉṟa ceytiyum riccarṭu kātil viḻuntē iruntatu. ivaṟṟaiyellām maṉattil vaittukkoṇṭu riccarṭaic cērntōr nārtamparlantu heṉṟi ākiyavariṉ naṭai mītuḷḷa cīṟṟattai veḷik kāṭṭātu aṭakkik koṇṭaṉar. riccarṭō, kaṭaliṉuḷ putaintu kaṉaṉṟu koṇṭirukkum vaṭavait tīppōṉṟu kumuṟik kumuṟi mēleḻuvatum aṭaṅki niṟpatumāka iruntāṉ.
vaṇaṅkāmuṭi maṉṉaṉāy niṟkum nārtamparlantai avaṉ eriyeḻa  uṟuttu nōkki, “aiya, tām aracar muṉṉilaiyil niṉṟu pēcukiṉṟīr niṉṟu pēcukiṉṟīr eṉpatai maṟakkac ceyta tamatu taṟperumai allatu tam talaivaratu taṟperumai yātō? allatu aracar yām allēm eṉṟukūṭa nīṅkaḷ uṅkaḷukkuḷḷākavē muṭivu kaṭṭiviṭṭīrkaḷē?” eṉṟāṉ.
taṉatu tiṭṭam muṟṟum aracar vāyiliruntu varuvatu kēṭṭu nārtamparlantu uḷḷūra makiḻcciyaṭaintum veḷikku oṉṟum aṟiyātavaṉ pōlac, “civaciva! eṉṉa coṟkaḷ coṉṉīr? kuṭikaḷākiya emakkut tamatu aracurimaiyai etirkkat tuṇivu varumō? yām vaṟitē em urimaikkāka maṉṟāṭa vantuḷḷōm. tām eṉ talaivar heṉṟi heripōrṭu pāliṅ purōk kōmakaṉukkuc ceyta tīṅkai akaṟṟi avarai ēṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭi maṭṭum vēṇṭukiṟōm” eṉṟāṉ.
tēṉ pūciya nañcē pōṉṟa avaṉatu nayavañcaka moḻikaḷ kēṭṭu riccarṭu tāṉ itukāṟum iyaṟṟiya piḻaikaḷai oru noṭi eṇṇip pārttu, “ām, iṉṟu varuntuvatāṟ payaṉeṉ? ipperumakkaḷ kuḻuvai varampil vaikkat tavaṟiṉēṉ. potumakkaḷiṉ nalleṇṇattaiyum ottuḻaippaiyum peṟat tavaṟiṉēṉ. nakattāṟ kiḷḷi  pakaivaṉ ippōtu kōṭariyāl taṟippiṉum taṟipaṭa muṭiyāta aḷavu vaḷarntu viṭṭāṉē! eṉ aracāṭci iruntavāṟu eṉ?” eṉṟu varuntiṉāṉ.
piṉ aṇaikaṭanta veḷḷattukku eṉ ceyvateṉṟu maṉantēṟi nārtamparlantai nōkki avaṉ “cari, um talaivaṉiṭam pōy aracaṉaik kāṇalām. kaṇṭu tam urimaikaḷaip peṟalām eṉṟu colka!” eṉṟāṉ.
ittuṭaṉ riccarṭiṉ nā niṉṟiruntālkūṭa riccarṭiṉ vīḻcci taṭaippaṭṭirukkalākum. riccarṭu pāliṅ purōkkiṉ perunilak kiḻamaiyai viṭṭukkoṭuttiruntāl, taṟkālikamāka vāvatu pālaṅ purōk taṉ paṭaikaḷaik kalaikkavēṇṭi vantirukkalām. āṉāl, periya tēriṉ pōkkai māṟṟum ciṟu caṟukkukkaṭṭai pōṉṟatoru nikaḻcci iccamayam riccarṭiṉ tīviṉaip payaṉāl nikaḻntu, avaṉatu tīya nāvait tūṇṭiṟṟu. veḷiyē ceṉṟu koṇṭirunta nārtamparlantu, ‘taṉ talaivar tara iṇakkam peṟavillaiyē’ eṉṟu niṉaittut tirumpi vantāṉ. ‘araṇṭavaṉ kaṇṇukku iruṇṭatellām pēy’ eṉṟapaṭi riccarṭukku ‘avaṉ taṉ maṟaippaiyum naṭippaiyum muṟṟilum tuṟantu nērē taṉ muṭivaik kēṭkiṟāṉō’ eṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu. tōṉṟavē, taṉ maṉa amaitiyaiyum pakuttaṟivaiyum caṟṟē paṟikoṭuttu viṭṭu, “ēṉ aiyaṉmīr, ittuṇai nāḷ viṭṭukkoṭuttatu pōtātu eṉṟā iṉṉum varukiṟīr? um talaivar, nāṉ evvaḷavu paṇintāl niṟaivaṭaivār? avarukku inta oru maṇimuṭi pōtumā? inta ōr aracirukkaiyum inta ōr araṇmaṉaiyum allatu inta oru nāṭum ulakamuntāṉ pōtumā? ivvaḷavum pōtāmal iruntāl inta riccarṭiṉ uyirē vēṇṭumāṉālum tarukiṟēṉ. atumaṭṭumaṉṟu; eṉ aracurimaiyōṭukūṭa eṉ taṉ matippuṅkūṭa vēṇṭumā? eṉṟu kēṭṭu varuvīr. nāṉ aracaṉāka vāḻāmal taṉi maṉitaṉeṉṟa muṟaiyilāvatu vāḻalāmā? inta iṅkilāntil eṉ maṉaiviyum nāṉ mitakkum nilamumāvatu eṉakkuriyavai eṉṟu nāṉ collik koḷḷalāmā? ivaiyaṉaittaiyum nīr aṟintu vantu collum!” eṉṟivvāṟu taṉ maṉakkotippaik kēḷvi kaḷāka vārttuk koṭṭiṉāṉ.
nārtamparlantu, “aracē! taṟpōtu tām taruvatākak kūṟuvataṉ oru pakuti pōtum. nāṉ em talaivaraik kalantukoṇṭu varukiṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi akaṉṟāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam akaṉṟa nārtamparlantu viraintu pāliṅ purōkkiṉiṭam ceṉṟu naṭanta vivaraṅkaḷaik kūṟiṉāṉ. riccarṭiṉiṭam paṭaivaṉmai illaiyeṉṟum, taṉ valimai yiṉmaiyait tāṉē uṇarntu  avaṉ kōpamum tāpamum aṭaikiṟāṉ eṉṟum kēṭṭatē. pāliṅ purōkkukkut taṉ etiriyiṉ vaṉmaiyiṟ pāti appōtē taṉ pakkam vantu viṭṭatākat tōṟṟiyatu. apperumaiyai avaṉ aṭakka āṟṟātu uṭaṉtāṉē nārtamparlantai nōkki, “riccarṭiṭam piṉṉum orumuṟai celka; ceṉṟu, muṟṟattil nāṉ niṟpatākavum iṟaṅki vantu pārkkat tiruvuḷam ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟum kūṟuka” eṉṟāṉ.
nārtamparlantu mūlam immoḻikaḷ riccarṭiṉ cevikaḷil pukavum, avaṉ, eṇcāṇ uṭalum oru cāṇākak kuṉṟiṉāṉ. kaṭalaik kuṭitta kuṟumuṉi pōla iccaṇṭāḷaṉaiyum, ivaṉait tāṅki niṉṟa ivviyaṉulakaiyumē viḻuṅki viṭuvōmā eṉṟa peruñciṉam eḻuntu avaṉaic cilanēram āṭpaṭuttiṟṟu. ciṉam tīrntapiṉ tuyar avaṉuḷḷattaik koḷḷai koṇṭatu. taṉ nilaimaiyaik kaṇṭu avaṉ alaṟiṉāṉ; aḻutāṉ; maṉam puḻuṅkip puḻuṅki vimmiṉāṉ. taṉ naṇparum paṇiyāḷarum tēṟṟa oruvāṟu tēṟiṉāṉ. iṟutiyiṟ kārlail talaimakaṉai nōkki, “antaṇāḷa, maṉṉikkavum; māntarai vīḻttum tuyar maṉṉaṉaiyum āḻttuvatu takātutāṉ. vāḻvil āṭci celuttiya ikkaṇkaḷ māḷvilum āṭci celuttum eṉak kāṭṭuvēṉ” eṉṟu eḻuntāṉ.
piṉ avaṉ nārtamparlantai ēṟeṭuttu nōkki ēḷaṉa nakaippuṭaṉ, “peruntakaiyōy, niṉ peruntakait talaivaṉiṭamiruntu nī koṇarnta māṟṟamum peruntakaimai uṭaiyatē. aracaṉ muṉ kuṭikaḷ varuvataṉṟik kuṭikaḷ muṉ aracar varuvatillaiyē! āyiṉum iḵtu uṅkaḷ kālam. nāṉ varukiṟēṉ; iṟaṅki varukiṟēṉ; kīḻ muṟṟattiṟkē kīḻnōkki iṟaṅki varukiṟēṉ; celka” eṉṟāḷ.
riccarṭiṉiṭam araikuṟai aṉpuṭaiyōr aṉaivarum aṟṟa kuḷattil aṟu nīrppaṟavai pōl uṟṟuḻittīrn tēkiṉār. oru cilar maṭṭumuṇmai naṭpukkilakkāyk ‘koṭṭiyum āmpalum neytalum pōla oṭṭi’ niṉṟaṉar. iṭukkaṇ eṉpatu, naṇparuḷ nallavar tīyavar uṇmai yuṭaiyavar pōliyāṉavar ivarkaḷaip pirittu vēṟupaṭuttum arippaṉṟō?
riccarṭiṉ vīḻcci uṟutippaṭṭu viṭṭatu! vīḻvatu iṉṟō nāḷaiyō eṉpatu maṭṭuntāṉ kēḷvi eṉṟa nilaiyilum, viṭātu niṉṟa aṉpurimaiyāḷar cilar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ “aracē! perun taṉmaiyaṟṟa inta heṉṟi pāliṅ purōkkiṉiṭam āttirappaṭṭut tam maṉattiluḷḷa taṉaittuṅ  kūṟiviṭa vēṇṭām. avaṉ āḷvaliyai vaittukkoṇṭu veḷimiraṭṭukku oru cākkut tēṭukiṟāṉ. muṉkūṭṭi avaṉ muṭivai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, avaṉukku avaṉ tēṭum cākkiṟku iṭaṅkoṭuttu viṭātīrkaḷ” eṉṟaṉar.
āṉāl, riccarṭiṉ maṉam vēṟu vaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟatu. muṉṉē piṉṉē taṉ muṭivu uṟuti eṉṟu avaṉukku aiyamaṟa viḷaṅkiṟṟu. tāṉa uralukkuḷ akappaṭṭavaṉē eṉṟu terintatu. koñcaṅ koñcamāy noṟuṅkavataiviṭa orē iṭiyāy iṭipaṭṭut takarntu poṭiyāvatu nallatallavā? pūṉaiyiṉ kaiyil  akappaṭṭa eli, appūṉai taṉṉaic caṟṟē viṭṭuviṭṭu viḷaiyāṭṭuk kāṭṭi vataikkaiyil, ataṟkiṭaṅ koṭukkum vakaiyil tappi ōṭap pārkkāmal appūṉaiyāl uṭaṉ kaṭittuk kollappaṭuvataṟkut tāṉē vaḻi pārppatu nallatallavā? ivveṇṇattuṭaṉ riccarṭu orupuṟam pāliṅ purōkkiṉ maṟainta vañca eṇṇaṅkaḷai veḷippaṭak kūṟik kōpa mūṭṭiṉāṉ. iṉṉoru puṟam avaṉ kūṟat tayaṅkiya moḻikaḷait tāṉē kūṟi, avaṉ vēlaiyai eḷitākkiṉāṉ.
5. muyaleyta ampum yāṉai piḻaitta vēlum
riccarṭaik kaṇṭatum pāliṅ purōk paṭam eṭukkappukum pāmpu paṇintu vaḷaivatupōl muḻaṅkāliṭṭu vaṇaṅki niṉṟāṉ. riccarṭu avaṉait tūkki niṟutti, “uḷḷattil illāta paṇivu uṭalil ēṉō?” eṉṟāṉ.
pāliṅ purōk tīṇṭappeṟṟa aravameṉac cīṟi, “aiyaṉē, uḷḷattiṟ paṇivu illaiyeṉak kūṟuvatu yār? taṉ urimaikkut tāṉ pōrāṭa varuvatutāṉ paṇi viṉmaiyō? ituvum tam aracāṭciyiṉ vaṇmai pōlum!” eṉṟāṉ.
itukēṭṭu riccarṭu mukam civantatu. kaṇkaḷ kaṉṟip paṉittaṉa. utaṭukaḷ paṭapaṭattaṉa. avaṉ pāliṅ purōkkai nōkki, “iṟaivaṉāl amarttappaṭṭa muṭiyaracu āṭciyaip paḻikkum tuṇivuṭaiya mēlōy! eṉ āṭciyai nīkki num āṭciyai niṟuvuvatāyiṉ niṟuvuka!” eṉṟāṉ.
pāliṅ purōkkiṉ mukam oḷi vīciṟṟu. kuralil oru perumitam eḻuntatu. āṉāl, māṟutalillāta veṟṟu moḻikaḷil avaṉ, “eṉakka uriyatai maṭṭumē yāṉ tammiṭam kōriṉēṉ” eṉṟu pacappiṉāṉ.
riccarṭiṉ ciṉam talaikāl teriyāmal tuḷḷik kutittatu. avaṉ tīviṉai avaṉ nāvil mīṇṭum vantu niṉṟatu. niṟkavē avaṉ, “aṉparīr, umakkuriyatum umakkut tantēṉ. eṉakkuriyatum umakkut tantēṉ. nāṉum iṉi um uṭaimai. iṉṉum eṉṉa vēṇṭum umakku? nāṉ ummuṭaṉ varavēṇṭumā? eṅka varavēṇṭum? laṇṭaṉukkā vara vēṇṭum?’ eṉap pala nāḷ kaṇakkil pāliṅ purōk paṭippaṭiyākak kēṭka irunta attaṉai ceytikaḷaiyum muṉkūṭṭi orē mūccil avaṉ muṉ aḷḷi vīciṉāṉ.
piṟar meyppukkāka vēṇṭā vēṇṭā eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu koṭuttataiyellām vāṅki vāṅkit tiṉṉum alakaikaḷ pōlap pāliṅ purōk, “avvaḷavu nāṉ eṇṇavillai! tāṅkaḷ avvaḷavum oruppaṭuvatāl aṅṅaṉamē ākuka. laṇṭaṉukkē puṟappaṭalām” eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭup pakkattil niṉṟōriṭam “riccarṭaiyum uṭaṉ koṇṭu laṇṭaṉ nōkkip payaṇamākuka” eṉṟāṉ.
oruvarum kavaṉikkāmalē riccarṭu aracaṉ, riccarṭu āyviṭṭāṉ.
pāliṅ purōk, aracaṉaic ciṟaiyāḷi pōla muṉṉē eḷiya uṭaiyil eḷiya ūrtiyil viṭṭuttāṉ nāṟpaṭaicūḻa araca uṭaiyuṭaṉ riccarṭiṉ kutiraiyilēṟi aṇimaṇikaḷuṭaṉ ūrvalam cellum aracaṉ pōṟ celvāṉāyiṉāṉ.
perumakkaḷ āṅkāṅku niṉṟu, “riccarṭu vīḻka, pāliṅpurōk oṅkuka” eṉak kattiṉar.
potumakkaḷ, palarum, koṭi piṭittavaṉukkuk kōvintāp pōṭumuṟaiyil kūṭiniṉṟu ‘pāliṅ purōk vāḻka, heṉṟi vāḻka’ eṉṟu toṇṭai kiḻiyak katti āravārittaṉar.
mānilamāḷum maṉṉavaṉukkinnilai vantuṟṟatē eṉap pirivu koṇṭavar mikaccilarē.
riccarṭiṉ pōkku iṅṅaṉamiruppak kaṟpaṇikalamum pirāṉciṉ utirnta meṉ malarumākiya avaṉaraci avaṉ ariya varavukkākak kāttiruntu kaṇ pañcaṭaintu ūṇum uṟakkamum  tuṟantu pēy pōlat taṉṉilai maṟantu nāṟpuṟamum ōṭik kaṇavaṉait tēṭuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. avaḷ nilai kaṇṭiraṅkiya cilar riccarṭukku nērnta paḻiyaiyum, laṇṭaṉukku avaṉ eṭuttuc cellappaṭum alaṅkōlak kāṭciyaiyuṅ kuṟittuk kūṟiṉar. kaṇavaṉiṭamē uyirvaittu naṭaippaṇimeṉac ciṉṉāḷāy vīṭṭil taṅkiyum ulaviyum vanta ammaṅkai nallāḷ, uyirai aṇaiya viraiyum uṭalē pōl, laṇṭaṉ nōkkiccellum vaḻiyiṟ cellalāṉāḷ.
riccarṭai uṭaṉkoṇṭu laṇṭaṉ ceṉṟa perumakkaḷ ūrvalam araciyaṟ perumaṉṟaṅkūṭumiṭamākiya 19vesṭ miṉisṭar ceṉṟu cērntatu. āṇṭu niṉṟum ‘heṉṟi vāḻka, pāliṅpurōk vāḻka’ eṉṟa kūkkural vāṉaip piḷantatu. riccarṭiṉ peyarait tāṉuṅ kūṟuvāraik kāṇōm. kuṭikaḷ aṉpiṉmai eṉṟa naccaravaṅ kaṇṭa riccarṭu. araciyal vāḻvil muṟṟilum veṟuppuṟṟu, ‘immakkaḷaiyō iv heṉṟiyaiyō iṉik kaṇṇiṟ kāṇātirukkum varam taruvāy’ eṉa iṟaivaṉai vapattalāṉāṉ.
piṉ maṇṇulaka vāḻvai veṟutta ammaṉṉaṉ yārum etirpārā vakaiyil nārtamparlantai aḻaittup, “periyīr, vaḷaintu vaḷaintu celvāṉēṉ? nērvaḻiyai nāṉ iṇakkattuṭaṉ kāṭṭukiṉṟēṉ. immaṇimuṭi eṉakku vēṇṭā. numtalaivar ataṉai ēṟkat tiruvuḷaṅ koḷḷiṉ ēṟṟaruḷumāṟu nāṉ iṟaiñcuvatākak kūṟuvīr” eṉṟāṉ.
kumpiṭappōṉa teyvam kuṟukkē varappeṟṟatu pōl makiḻveytip pāliṅ purōk, riccarṭait tāṉē vantu araciyal maṉṟil ataṉai viṭṭuk koṭukkumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉāṉ.
iṟappiṉum māṉam tuṟavā āṅkila aracar vaḻiyil vanta riccarṭu, “yāṉ taṟpōtu aracu tuṟantu aracurimaip cāmpaleṉa niṟpiṉum, yāṉ kuṭikaḷ muṉceṉṟu kuṭimakkaḷ nilaiyil pēca oruppaṭēṉ” eṉṟu maṟuttāṉ. heṉṟiyum, māḷum ariyēṟṟiṉiṭam māḷumuṉ etirppatu tavaṟeṉak koṇṭu, taṉatu vēṇṭukōḷai vaṟpuṟuttāmal caṟṟup piṉvāṅkiṉāṉ.
riccarṭu ataṉpiṉ, aracirukkaiyum, muṭiyum tāṉ heṉṟiyiṉiṭam oppuvippatākavum, tāṉ pirāṉcukkuc cella virumpuvatākavum colliyaṉuppiṉāṉ.
koṭuppōr kulapperumai aṟiyāta vāṅkuvōr kulat tōṉṟalākiya heṉṟi, riccarṭiṉ kōrikkaikaḷuḷ muṉṉatai maṭṭum veṭṭaveḷiccamāka maṉṟattār muṉ vācittuk kāṭṭip piṉṉatai aṭakkik koṇṭāṉ.
heṉṟi pāliṅ purōk, perumakkaḷ talaivaṉāka yārk kōmakaṉ tuṇai cellak, kāṉripert talaimakaṉ vaḻikāṭṭa, aracirukkaiyēṟi, mēḷatāḷaṅkaḷukkiṭaiyē maṇimuṭi aṇivikkap peṟṟāṉ.
20talaimakkaḷ, 21perumakkaḷ, 22potumakkaḷ ākiya 23mummaṇṭalatta varum pōṟṟa, nāṉilameṅkum pukaḻalai vīca, “pāliṅ purōk vāḻka. nāṉkām heṉṟi vāḻka” eṉṟa āravāraṅkaḷukkiṭaiyē aṉṟu heṉṟi muṭicūṭṭap peṟṟāṉ.
riccarṭu eṉṉum ñāyiṟu araciyal vāṉil mēlpāl kaṇkavar ciṟappuṭaṉum nallōr maṉattuyarākiya niḻaṟpaṭaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum maṟaiyavum, āṅka vaḷarpiṟai mutaṟ kalaipōlum perumitattuṭaṉ heṉaṟi eṉṉum matikkoḻuntu mēlēḻuntatu.
6. tōlviyil veṟṟi
putiya muṭicūṭṭu viḻāvil kaṟai oṉṟē oṉṟutāṉ. ‘pulaṉaḻukkaṟṟa antaṇāḷa’ ṉākiya kārlail talaimakaṉ araciyal maṉṟil muṭicūṭṭa muṉ eḻuntu, “aracar eṉa muṭicūṭṭap peṟappōkum pāliṅ purōk  perumakaṉārē! aracar aracirukkai ēṟuvatum tuṟappatum makkaḷ viruppu veṟuppālaṉṟu; iṟaivaṉ tiruvuḷap pōkku oṉṟiṉāl maṭṭumē ākak kūṭiyatu eṉṟu aṟivīr. riccarṭukkuriya ivvaracurimaiyai avaṉiṭamiruntu paṟikkavō, mēṟkoḷḷavō umakku eṉṉa takuti uṇṭu? iṟaivaṉ muṉṉilaiyil riccarṭē aracar; aturuvē eṉ koḷkai; piṟa aṟivuṭaiyōr koḷkaiyum atuvēyām. umakkañci ummai ēṟkum kōḻaikaḷukkup puṟampāṉa makkaḷum uḷar. avarē makkaḷ. piṟar mākkaḷē eṉpatai nīr uṇarka” eṉṟāṉ.
pāliṅ purōk poṟumaiyiḻantu, muṭicūṭṭu viḻānāḷ eṉṟu kūṭa eṇṇātu, “uṭaṉ tāṉē ippārppāṉ talaiyait tuṇikka” eṉṟu paṇittāṉ. muṭicūṭṭiṉ mutaṟpali niṟaivēṟiṟṟu.
iṉi riccarṭiṉ kolai oṉṟutāṉ kuṟai.
riccarṭiṉ tuṇaivi 24laṇṭaṉukku vantu avaṉ maṇikkūṇṭuc ciṟaikkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭuvatākak kēṭṭu, avviṭattukkuc cellum vaḻiyil ōriṭattil taṉ uyirt tōḻiyar cilaruṭaṉ kāttiruntāḷ
avaṉ ciṟaiyāḷipōl koṇṭu varappaṭum appōtum, pukaḻiṟku māṟāka avaṉ ikaḻmālai cūṭṭappeṟṟu varum atē nērattiluṅkūṭa, avaḷ kaṇṇukku avaṉ aṭimutal muṭivarai ovvōr aṅkulamum niṟaiyaracākavē kāṇappaṭṭāṉ.
avaṉ tuyarāl avaṉ vaṉappum kavarcciyum avaḷ kaṇkaḷukkup paṉmaṭaṅku mikuntu tōṉṟiṉa. avaḷ taṉ tōḻiyar tōḷiṟ cāyntu avaṉaik kuṟitta nōkkuṭaṉ, “ā, maṉṉar eṉṟa collukkoru makuṭampōl viḷaṅkum eṉ iṟaivaṉai nōkkuvīr! tuyarāl kaṟaippaṭṭa am makuṭattai yāṉ eṉ kātaṟkaṇṇīrāl kaḻuvi iṉṉum puttuyir peṟumpaṭi putuppippēṉ” eṉṟu pulampiṉāḷ.
avaḷ tuyarkaṇṭu taṉ uḷḷuyir pocuṅkiṉum avaḷaiyum avaḷ tuyaraiyum kaṇṭum kāṇātavaṉ pōṉṟa tōṟṟattuṭaṉ riccarṭu avaḷait tōḻiyaruṭaṉ cēra nōkki, ‘yāṉ uṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkum kālam aṉṟu atu. iṅkilāntiṉ puyal kāṟṟum eṉ muṭivum uṅkaḷuk kukantavaiyalla. innāṭṭiṉ cūṟāvaḷiyiṉ cuḻaṟciyuṭ paṭāmal amaitiyuṭaṉ pirāṉcu nāṭu ceṉṟu kaṉṉiyar maṭattuṭ ceṉṟu otuṅki uṟaiyuṅkaḷ” eṉṟāṉ.
eṉ talaivar tāṅkarum tuyariṉum eṉṉai iṅṅaṉam maṟattal takumō? nāṉ tam uṭaliṉum uyiriṉum oru pakuti eṉṟa muṟaiyil tam tuyaraiyum pakirntu tuykkum urimai yuṭaiyavaḷaṉṟō?” eṉṟāḷ appeṇmai araci.
riccarṭu miṉṉiyiṭittārttu maḻai poḻintu veḷittālaṉṉa mukattuṭaṉ avaḷ pakkam tirumpi nōkki, “eṉ uyirē! nī eṉ pakuti maṭṭilum alla; eṉ uḷḷuyir nī; uyir nilai nī; uṉṉait tākkāta ettuyarukkum ettakaiya muṭivukkum nāṉ añcēṉ. āṉāl, uṉṉait tākkum tuyar eṉṉaik kōḻaiyākki viṭum. nī ṉ tuyarai pakirvataṟku māṟāka, ataṉaip paṉmaṭaṅk mikaippaṭutta maṭṭumē ceyyakkūṭum. ātaliṉ ivvuṭal ennilai eytiṉum ataṉāl tākkuṟāmal ataṉ uyirāka vilaki niṉṟu maṟupiṟappil eṉṉuṭaṉ vantu cērvāy” eṉṟāṉ.
araci accoṟ kēṭṭup piṉṉum urukiṉāḷ. piṉ avaṉ pakkam pārātu nārtamparlantu pakkam nōkki, “eṉṉaiyum ciṟaiyāḷiyāka uṭaṉ koṇṭēka vēṇṭukiṟēṉ” eṉṟāḷ.
nārtamparlantu kaṭukaṭutta mukattuṭaṉ, “ammaṇi! tam kaṇavar ippōtu ciṟaiyāḷiyum araciyal pakaivarum āvar. avaruṭaṉ celvatu muṭiyāta kāriyam. atu maṭṭumaṉṟu, avar ippōtu nāṭṭup pakaivarāṉa paṭiyāl avaruṭaṉ pēcuvatum parivu koḷvatum kūṭa, araciyaṟ pakaimaiyākak karutappaṭa iṭamuṇṭu” eṉṟāṉ.
nākkil narampaṟṟup pēcappaṭṭa immoḻikaḷ kēṭṭuppoṅkiya ciṉattai uḷḷaṭakkik koṇṭu araci piṉṉum, “maṉṉarai ākkavum aḻikkavum valla maṉṉar maṉṉarē! nāṉ avaruṭaṉ kāṭṭum parivu araciyal muṟaippaṭi maṉṉaṉ eṉṟa vakaiyil allavē, peṇ eṉṟa muṟaippaṭi kaṇavaṉ eṉṟa vakaiyiltāṉē! taṉatu putu maṉṉaṉiṭam tam blvākkaip payaṉpaṭutti, eṉ kaṇavaṉukku ittuyar poḻutil tuṇaiyāyc cila nāṭkaḷēṉum utava iṇakkam aḷikkalākātā?” eṉṟu maṉṟāṭiṉāḷ.
nārtamparlantu irakkamiṉṟi, “itaṟku maṉṉaṉiṭam cella vēṇṭuvatillai. muṭiyāteṉṟu nāṉ kūṟiviṭṭēṉ” eṉṟu taṉṉāṇmaiyuṭaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ.
taṉatu putiya valimaiyiṉ veṟiyāl riccarṭu avaḷukkut taṉ iṟuti muttamaḷittu viṭaikoḷvataik kūṭa avaṉ taṭuttāṉ. appōtu araciyiṉ mukam carēleṉṟu civantu kaṟuttatu. uyiriṉum iṉiya taṉ tuṇaivikku nērnta avamatippukkaṇṭu riccarṭu cīṟiṉāṉ.
cīṟṟattāl oru vāḻnāḷiṉ aracurimai vīṟumuṟṟum oru noṭikkuḷ avaṉ mukattilum nilaiyiṉum vantēṟa, avaṉ nārtamparlantai aḻaleḻa nōkkik kēṭpōr añcum vañciṉa moḻikaḷ pakaralāṉāṉ.
“koṇṭa muṭi maṉṉaṉukku vañcakam ceyta nī, koṇṭa kaṇavaṉiṭamiruntu maṉaiviyaip pirikka eṇṇutal iyalpuṭaiyatē!
“muṭi maṉṉaṉukku vañcamiḻaitta nī, avvañcattiṉ payaṉāka uyarttiya niṉ kaippiṭi maṉṉaṉukku vañca miḻaippāy!
“maṇimuṭiyārvattāl tīṅkiḻaitta niṉ talaivaṟku, ammuṭi muḷmuṭiyāki avaṉukkut tuṉpattaiyum veṟuppaiyun taruvatāka!
“muṭimaṉṉar kuṭikketirāka mēlōṅkiya niṉ puṉkai niṉ kuṭiyaiyuṅ keṭukka!” eṉṟāṉ.
ippaḻi moḻikaḷaip poruṭpaṭuttātu nārtamparlantu cirikka muyaṉṟāṉ. āṉāl, atu pēyccirippaiyottu nakaiyiḻantiruntatu. maṉṉaṉuṭaṉ uḷḷūrap parivu koṇṭavarkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷaiyum immoḻikaḷ īrttaṉa.
aṉaivarum pēccu mūcciṉṟi niṟpa, riccarṭum araciyum vāyaṟṟa neṭum pārvaiyuṭaṉum neṭumūccuṭaṉum oruvarai oruvar pirintaṉar. itu mutal riccarṭiṉ vāḻvu, kuṟukiya maṇikkūṇṭuc ciṟaiyiṉum; araciyiṉ vāḻvu, akaṉṟa payaiṟṟa maṭattu vāḻvilum kaḻiyalāyiṉa.
araci, taṉ vāḻnāḷai neṭunāḷ tāṅka iyalātu uṭal vāṭi vataṅki ulakai nīttāḷ. avaḷ uyir taṉ talaivaṉai etirkoḷḷa muṉkūṭṭi viṇṇulakeytiṟṟu.
riccarṭiṉ āṭci muṭivu kēṭṭup potumakkaḷiṟ palar viyappuṟṟaṉar. avaṉ āṭciyiṉ kuṟaipāṭukaḷai naṉku uṇarntavarkaḷkūṭa, avaṉ āṭci muṭivu ēṟpuṭaiya taṉṟeṉak koṇṭaṉar. ‘niḻaliṉ arumai veyilil teriyum’ eṉṟapaṭi perumakkaḷum, iṉpa vāḻvum taṟporumaiyuṭaiya riccarṭiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷ, taṉṉalamē nōkkamākak koṇṭa heṉṟi āṭciyiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷaip pārkkiṉum evvaḷavō mēl eṉṟu eṇṇiṉar.
riccarṭukku uṭpakaiyāy niṉṟa yārk kōmakaṉ makaṉ āmerla perumakaṉ talaimaiyil pala perumakkaḷum, potumakkaḷum cērntu, tirumpa riccarṭai aracaṉākkum eṇṇattuṭaṉum cūḻcci ceytaṉar. riccarṭukku vañca miḻaitta yārk, maintaṉaiyum maṉaiviyaiyum kūṭak kāṭṭikkoṭuttu nalla peyareṭukka muyaṉṟāṉ.
heṉṟiyiṉ camaya cañcīvikaḷil oru mūvar yārkkaiviṭa orupaṭi muṉ ceṉṟu ciṟaiyilirunta riccarṭai valiyac caṇṭaikkiḻuttup paṭukolai ceytaṉar.
taṉṉalattiṟkāka riccarṭiṟku etirāka aṉaivaraiyum tūṇṭi heṉṟi, riccarṭiṉ peruntaṉmaiyiṉaiyum, avaṉukketirākat taṉ tūṇṭutalukkiṇaṅkiya vañcakarkaḷiṉ ciṟumaiyaiyum uḷḷapaṭi uṇarntu riccarṭai maṉamārap pukaḻntu. avaṉ kolaiñarkaḷait tūkkaliṭṭāṉ. heṉṟiyiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷai icceyal oruvāṟu potu makkaḷ muṉṉilaiyil kaḻuviṟṟu eṉṉalām.
itē kāraṇattāl avaṉ yārk kōmakaṉ coṟkēḷātu avaṉ maṉaivi makkaḷai maṉṉittu viṭṭāṉ. yārkkiṉ kōḻaittaṉamāṉa naṭpai viṭa, avarkaḷatu vīrañceṟinta pakaimai nallateṉak koṇṭāṉ. ataṟkēṟpa, avarkaḷum riccarṭiṭam kāṭṭiya uṇmaiyiṉai avaṉiṭamuṅ kāṭṭiṉar.
heṉṟi iṅṅaṉam riccarṭukkut taṉṉalattāl tāṉ iḻaitta tīṅkaic cariceyya eṇṇiyum, riccarṭiṉ pukaḻum vañciṉamum avaṉ vāḻkkaiyai vāḷpōl īrttaṉa.
riccarṭukku vāḻvillāta veṟṟi, māṇṭapiṉ kiṭṭiyateṉṉalām. avaṉ muṭiyaracai veṉṟavaṉaiyum avaṉ vaḻiyiṉaraiyum riccarṭiṉ peyar niṉṟu neṭunāḷ varuttip palikoṇṭu piṉ taṇintu, iṅkilāntukku māṟākap pukaḻāka māṟiṟṟu.
āṅkila makkaḷ avaṉ araciyal kuṟaikaḷai maṟantu, avaṉatu kuṇa niṟaivaiyum tuyariṭaiyē avaṉ kāṭṭiya poṟaiyaiyum perun taṉmaiyiṉaiyum neṭunāḷ pukaḻntu pārāṭṭiṉar.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ

8.	Duke of York  	20. Clergyc

to.pē.: 24339030, cel: 9444410654
miṉṉañcal: naṭayaeyaṇāyapayavēa@paayaṭai.uṭia
 tāḷ : 16.0 ki. mēplittō,  aḷavu : 1/8 temmi 
 eḻuttu : 11.5 puḷḷi,  pakkam : 336
kaṭṭamaippu : iyalpu  paṭikaḷ : 500 
 nūlākkam : kō. cittirā 
 aṭṭai vaṭivamaippu : celvaṉ pā. ari (hariṣ) 
  accu : veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ,  
āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉai - 600 006. 

nuḻaivurai
tamiḻiṉattiṟkut tam iṉa uṇarvaiyum, moḻiyuṇarvaiyum ūṭṭi vaḷarttavarkaḷ tantai periyār, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā, taṉittamiḻ iyakkattiṉ tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyār, pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār mutalāṉa perumakkaḷ palarāvar.
paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyār iravu pakal pārātu uḻaittu eḻutiya nūlkaḷ 120 kkum mēṟpaṭṭavai (avaṟṟuḷ āṅkila nūlkaḷ aintu). eṅkaḷ kaikaḷukkuk kiṭaitta nūlkaḷ 97. avaṟṟaip poruḷvaḻip pirittuk kāla varicaippaṭutti 48 tokutikaḷāka appātturaiyam eṉum talaippil tamiḻ ulakukku vaḻaṅkukiṟōm.
tamiḻiṉam taṉ nilaiyuṇarat tavaṟiya vēḷaiyil tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyaiyum, tamiḻiṉattiṉ paṇṭaip perumaiyaiyum kāppataṟkut  takka vaḻikāṭṭi amaippukaḷāka 1916il toṭaṅkappaṭṭavai taṉittamiḻ iyakkamum, tirāviṭar iyakkamum ākum. ivviru iyakkaṅkaḷiṉ paṅkaḷippāl tamiḻiṉam eḻuccipeṟṟatu. ivvuṇmaiyai it ittokuppukaḷaip payilvōr eḷitāy uṇara muṭiyum.
tamiḻmoḻi āyvālum, tamiḻaka varalāṟṟu āyvālum, moḻi peyarpput tiṟattālum paṉmoḻip pulamaiyālum tamiḻiṉ mēṉmaikkup perum paṅkāṟṟiyavar appātturaiyār. 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷuḷ mutal varicaiyil vaittup pōṟṟappaṭupavar avar. avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyaikkuṟittu periyavar mukam māmaṇi avarkaḷum, pērāciriyar ku.ve. pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ avarkaḷum tattam nūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkiya varicaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi appātturaiyam tokuppukaḷai veḷiyiṭukiṟōm.
tamiḻakam muḻuvatum alaintu, perumuyaṟciyāl tēṭiccēkarittavai inta 97 nūlkaḷ. eṅkaḷukkuk kiṭaikkāta nūlkaḷaip piṉṉiṇaippil cērttuḷḷōm. annūlkaḷ vaittiruppōr vaḻaṅkiṉāl  naṉṟiyuṭaṉ aṭutta patippil cērttu veḷiyiṭuvōm. it tokuppukaḷil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa nūlkaḷai uruvākkit tamiḻar kaikaḷil tavaḻa viṭuvataṟkut tokuppāciriyar muṉaivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār avarkaḷum, yāṉum peṟṟa iṭarppāṭukaḷ mikuti. arumai makaḷ muṉaivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār tam tokuppuraiyil ittokuppukaḷ evvāṟu uruvākkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatai virivāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
appātturaiyāriṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷait tamiḻkūṟum nallulakukku vaḻaṅkiya patippakaṅkaḷil mutaṉmaiyāṉatu caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam. tamiḻ-tamiḻar mūlaṅkaḷait tamiḻakam tēṭippaṭippataṟku aṭittaḷamāka amainta patippakam caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam.
pāri nilaiyam, maṇivācakar patippakam, vaḷḷuvar paṇṇai, pūmpukār patippakam, vacantā patippakam, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam muliya pala patippakaṅkaḷ ipperuntamiḻ aṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻukku vaḷamum valimaiyum cērttuḷḷaṉa. 
innūlkaḷiṉ tokuppāciriyar pērāciriyar muṉaivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār avarkaḷ, tamiḻāyvuk kaḷattil kuṟippiṭattakkavar. tamiḻāyvup parappiṟku valimaiyūṭṭa ivarukkup pallāṟṟāṉum utavivarupavar ivaruṭaiya arumaik kaṇavar maruttuvar cēkkiḻār avarkaḷ. tamiḻppatippulakam, ivviṇaiyariṉ tamiḻkkāppup pēruḻaippai eṉṟum niṉaivu kūrum.
tokuppu nūlkaḷiṉ uḷḷaṭakkam ceppamāka uruvāvataṟkut taṉakkuḷḷa uṭal nalivaiyum tāṅkik koṇṭu uḻaitta tamiḻmakaḷ 
kō. cittirāvukku naṉṟi. tokuppukaḷiṉ mukappu aṭṭaikaḷ pala vaṇṇa vaṭivamaippuṭaṉ varuvataṟku uḻaitta celvaṉ. pā. ari (hariṣ) uḻaippiṟku nalla etirkālam uṇṭu.  it tokuppukaḷ ellā nilai
yilum naṉṟāka varuvataṟku uḷḷum puṟamum uḻaittu utaviyavar 
tiru. irā. paramēcuvaraṉ. patippucciṟappiṟku utaviya tiru. taṉacēkaraṉ, 
tiru. ku. marutu, tiru. vi. matimāṟaṉ innālvarum naṉṟikkuriyōr.
ittokuppukaḷil iṭampeṟṟuḷḷa nūlkaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭic cērttavai. kaṉṉimārā nūlakattil irunta nūlkaḷaip paṭiyeṭuttu utaviya `kaṉṉimārā’ nūlakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷukkum `civakurunātaṉ centamiḻ nūl nilaiyam’ (kumpakōṇam), tāḷāḷar pērā. muṉaivar irāma kurunātaṉ avarkaḷukkum neñcam niṟainta naṉṟi. ceṉṉai taramaṇiyil iyaṅki varum rōcā muttaiyā nūlakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ  utavikku naṉṟi. 
nūlkaḷai meyppup pārttu utaviyavar perumpulavar ayyā paṉacai aruṇā avarkaḷ. muṉaivar aru. apirāmi taṉ āciriyap paṇikkiṭaiyilum cōrvuṟātu patippakam vantu it tokuppukaḷ veḷivaruvataṟku ellā nilaiyilum utaviyavar. mēlum ittokuppukaḷ naṉṟāka veḷivaruvataṟku utaviyavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ taṉip pakkattil pativu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
taṉakkeṉa vāḻātu, tamiḻkkeṉa vāḻntu, pallāṇṭuk kālam uḻaitta  paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyāriṉ nūlkaḷai appātturaiyam eṉum talaippil tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kaikaḷil tavaḻa viṭuvatil perumakiḻcci aṭaikiṟōm.
“ākkattai eṉakkin nāṭṭār
  aḷittiṭṭa aṟivai yellām 
  tēkkieṉ tamiḻmēṉ maikkē
  celaviṭak kaṭamaip paṭṭēṉ.”
 - pāvēntar

kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ
tokuppurai
maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷāriṭam paṭṭai tīṭṭap peṟṟa
taṉmāṉat tamiḻaṟiñar!
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻ aṟivāḷumaikaḷil perum pulamaiyāḷar paṉmoḻippulavar kā. appātturaiyār. inti moḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarāyt toṭaṅkiyatu avariṉ vāḻkkai. piṉpu tamiḻ, tamiḻar, tamiḻnāṭu eṉṉum cintaṉaiyil īṭupaṭṭār; nuṭpamāṉa palvēṟu āyvu nūlkaḷai eḻutiyum piṟa moḻikaḷil iruntu (ilakkiyam, āyvu, aṟiñarkaḷiṉ cintaṉaikaḷ pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ) tamiḻil moḻi peyarttum tamiḻiḻulakukku vaḻaṅkiṉār. avar nūlkaḷ tamiḻ āyvup parappil perum nalviḷaivukaḷai ēṟpaṭuttiṉa.
“avar tamiḻiṉ mūlattaiyē ārāya muṉaintavar. tamiḻiṉattiṉ varalāṟṟait turuvi turuvi ārāyvataṉ mūlam tamiḻ iṉattiṟkum maṟṟa iṉattiṟkum iṭaiyē tōḻamaiyai ēṟpaṭutta naṟpaṇi ceytirukkiṟār”  pēraṟiñar aṇṇā paṉmoḻip pulavariṉ āyvut taṉmaiyai ippaṭi  eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār.
pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār, paṉmoḻip pulavaraiyum, pāvāṇaraiyum oppiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu maṉaṅkoḷattakkatu. tēvanēyappāvāṇaraiyum - kā. appātturaiyāraiyum kuṟippiṭum pōtu, “ivviruvarum irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭup pulamaikku iraṇṭu mērumalaikaḷ; maṟainta kumarik kaṇṭattu ōṭiyirunta paḵṟuḷiyāṟum kumariyāṟum pōṉṟavarkaḷ; kaḻakap pulavaruḷ paraṇarum kapilarum pōṉṟa perumakkaḷ; moḻiyaiyum iṉattaiyum tūkki niṟutta vanta nuṇṇaṟi vāḷarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kālattu maṟṟa piṟa pulavarkaḷ viṇmīṉkaḷ eṉṟāl, ivarkaḷ iruvarum katiravaṉum nilavum pōṉṟa antaṇarkaḷ; centamiḻ aṟavōrkaḷ; toṇṭu tavam iyaṟṟiya tīntamiḻt tuṟavōrkaḷ. moḻippaṟṟum, iṉappaṟṟum, nāṭṭuppaṟṟum koṇṭa nal uravōrkaḷ.” tamiḻulakiṟku appātturaiyār āṟṟiya paṇiyiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyaiyum avaruṭaiya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tēvaiyaiyum peruñcittiraṉār ivvāṟu uṇarttukiṟār.
camūkam palvēṟu kūṟukaḷai uḷḷaṭakkic ceyalpaṭṭuk koṇṭirukkum. acceyalpāṭukaḷ cariyāṉa vakaiyil amaintu uriya puḷḷiyil iṇaiyum poḻutu, ac camūkam mēleḻukiṟatu. pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutikkālattil tamiḻakattil appaṭiyāṉa oṉṟaik kaṭṭiyamaikka vēṇṭiya nilai iruntatāl, ataṉ toṭaracciyāṉa ceyalpāṭukaḷum eḻuntaṉa. 
-	taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tōṟṟam
-	nītik kaṭci toṭakkam
-	nāṭṭu viṭutalai uṇarcci
-	tamiḻiṉa urimai eḻucci
-	pakuttaṟivu viḻippuṇarcci
-	intit tiṇippu etirppup pōr
-	putiya kalvi muṟaip payiṟci
-	putuvakai ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ aṟimukam
ippaṭip palvēṟu taḷaṅkaḷil tamiḻakam taṉṉai maṟu kaṭṭamaippuc ceyya muṉaintukoṇṭiruntatu. ataṟkāṉa ottuḻaippum ceyaṟpāṭukaḷum palvēṟu nilaikaḷil tuṇaiyāka amaintaṉa.  appātturaiyāriṭam inti āciriyar - intit tiṇippu etirppu, pakti cārpu  -  pakuttaṟivuc cintaṉai, kāṅkiracuk koḷkai - tirāviṭak koḷkai, marapilakkiyam - navīṉa ilakkiyam eṉṉum muraṇnilaikaḷ iruntālum, “tamiḻ, tamiḻar, tamiḻnāṭu” eṉṉum taḷattil uṟutiyākac ceyalpaṭṭār. maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, tēvanēyappāvāṇariṉ cintaṉaikaḷai uṭceṟittu, valuvāṉa karuttākkaṅkaḷai uruvākkiṉār. avai tamiḻiṉattiṉ maṟumalarccikku ūṉṟukōlāy amaintaṉa. 
“tāymoḻiyum, tāymoḻi ilakkiyamum, tāymoḻik kalviyumē maṉita nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭai eṉpatu uṇarappaṭāmal intiyā neṭunāḷ vāḻa muṭiyātu. tamiḻar ivvuṇmaiyai aṟintu tamiḻaṟivum ulaka aṟivum oruṅkē peṟa utavum eṇṇam koṇṭē tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟṟilē ārvam ēṟpaṭāta ikkālattil ulaka varalāṟu eḻuta muṟpaṭṭōm” eṉpatu avar kūṟṟu, iṉṟum annilai muḻutāy uṇarappaṭāmal uḷḷatai eṉṉa colvatu!
appātturaiyār toṭakkattil intiya tēciyap pēriyakkattukkuḷ tammai iṇaittuk koṇṭu, kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ koḷkaikaḷai ēṟṟār. ataṉaṭippaṭaiyil kānti rattiṉa tiruppukaḻ, kānti purāṇam, tāḻttap paṭṭōr kōyil nuḻaivu viḻā mutalāṉa pāṭalkaḷai itaḻkaḷil eḻutiṉār. kāṅkiracu muṉ vaitta moḻikkoḷkai kuṟippākat tamiḻakattil intiyait tiṇikka muṟpaṭṭa muyaṟci, tamiḻakattil perum kontaḷippai uruvākiyatu. inta nikaḻvu (1938 - 1939) avarait tamiḻar tēciyam nōkkit tiruppiyatu. ataṉāl periyāriṉ cuyamariyātai, pakuttaṟivuc cintaṉaikaḷōṭu tammai iṇaittukkoṇṭār. tamatu nilaippāṭṭai, avarē kūṟukiṟār. “car.ē. irāmacāmi mutaliyār pōṉṟavarkaḷ toṭakkattil cuyamariyātai iyakkattai ātarittup piṉ vilaka nērntatu. intak kālaṅkaḷil kāṅkiracai viṭṭō, caiva iyakkaṅkaḷai viṭṭō, tamiḻ iyakkaṅkaḷai viṭṭō vilakāmal niṉṟu, ellā muṟpōkku  vīrarkaḷaiyum iṇaikka nāṉ muyaṉṟēṉ. periyār itaṉai etirkkavillai. taṉmāṉa iyakkattiṟkum, tirāviṭa iyakkattukkum, tamiḻ iyakkattukkum eṉṉuṭaiya nilai iṉṟuvarai payaṉpaṭṭē vantuḷḷatu” - (aṟivuc curaṅkam, pak.100,101) paṉmoḻip pulavar oru kuṟippiṭṭa ellaikkuḷ (iyakkam cārntum eḻuttu cārntum) niṉṟuviṭāta urimaiyuṇarviṉar!
paṉmoḻip pulavar peyarāl veḷivanta mutal nūl kumarikkaṇṭam (1940-43). itu moḻipeyarppu nūl. kāḻi. kaṇṇucāmi piḷḷai cila pakkaṅkaḷ moḻipeyarttu iruntatai, muḻumaiyāka ivar moḻipeyarttuḷḷār. itaṉ toṭarcciyāka,
- 	ulaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷai, varalāṟukaḷait tamiḻil moḻi peyarttal.
- 	tamiḻ moḻi, iṉam toṭarpāṉa āṅkila nūlkaḷait tamiḻil tarutal.
 -	tamiḻ moḻi, iṉam, nāṭu cārnta cintaṉaiyākkaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkal.
- 	tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷai ulaka ilakkiyaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭu nōkki tamiḻ ilakkiyattiṉ ciṟappai uṇarttal.
- 	tirukkuṟaḷukku mika virivāṉa viḷakkavurai varaital.
- 	nuṇ viḷakkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ palvakai akarāti tokuttal.
inta aṭippaṭaiyil avaruṭaiya nūlkaḷ toṭarntu veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. 1947 - 1949 ām āṇṭukaḷil naṭuvaṇ araciṉ ceytit tuṟaiyil paṇiyāṟṟiya poḻutu, intiyāvil moḻic cikkal eṉṉum nūlai eḻutiṉār. innūlukku maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ 40 pakka aḷavil muṉṉurai vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. innūl eḻutiyataṉ kāraṇamāka avaratu aracup paṇi paṟikkappaṭṭatu.
 paṇiyiṉṟi irunta (1949 - 1959) kālakkaṭṭaṅkaḷil nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷai eḻutiyuḷḷār. ivaruṭaiya nūlkaḷaic caivacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, pārinilaiyam, āciriyar nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, vaḷḷuvar paṇṇai, alamēlu patippakam pōṉṟa patippakaṅkaḷum piṟavum  veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻmaṇ patippakam ippōtu aṉaittu nūlkaḷaiyum 48 tokutikaḷāka veḷiyiṭum arumpaṇiyai niṟaivēṟṟiyuḷḷatu.
ulaka nākarikattiṉ vittu tamiḻ eṉat tam nuṇṇāyviṉ vaḻi niṟuviya, paṉmoḻip pulavariṉ aṉaittu nūlkaḷum tokukkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟa vēṇavāviṉāl tamiḻmaṇ patippaka niṟuvaṉar aiyā iḷavaḻakaṉār it tokuppiṉai uruvākkap paṇittārkaḷ. aiyā avarkaḷ tamiḻukku āṟṟum pēruḻaippu eṉṉai viyakkac ceyyum. meyvaruttam pārāmal kaṇtuñcāmal eṭutta ceyalai nērttiyōṭu ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatil uṟutiyāka niṟpavar. avaruṭaṉ iṇaintu ittokuppiṉai uruvākkuvatil makiḻcciyaṭaikiṟēṉ.
it tokuppiṟkāṉa nūlkaḷ kumpakōṇam centamiḻ nūlakam, rōcā muttaiyā nūlakam, kaṉṉimārā nūlakam, veṟṟiyaraci patippakam mutalāṉa  iṭaṅkaḷil iruntu tiraṭṭappeṟṟaṉa. pērāciriyar muṉaivar ku.ve. pāla cuppiramaṇiyam avarkaḷiṭamiruntum cila nūlkaḷ peṟappaṭṭaṉa. kiṭaitta nūlkaḷ 97. avai 48 tokutikaḷākat tokukkappaṭṭu veḷi varukiṉṟaṉa. at tokutikaḷ kīḻkkāṇum muṟaikaḷil pakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
1.	tamiḻ - tamiḻar - tamiḻnāṭu
2.	varalāṟu
3.	āyvukaḷ
4.	moḻipeyarppu
5.	iḷaiyōr kataikaḷ
6.	potu nilai
perumpāṉmai nūlkaḷ ittokuppiṟkuḷ ataṉataṉ poruḷ aṭippaṭaiyilēyē tokukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pakkac camanilai karuti māṟṟam peṟṟum uḷḷaṉa. vevvēṟu patippakaṅkaḷ orē nūlai vēṟu vēṟu peyarkaḷil veḷiyiṭṭiruntaṉa. cila nūlkaḷiṉ mutal patippu kiṭaikkāta nilai! kiṭaitta patippukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyilēyē nūlkaḷ tokukkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. mukam māmaṇi avarkaḷiṉ aṟivuccuraṅkam appātturaiyār eṉṟa nūlaiyum, pērāciriyar muṉaivar ku.ve. pāla cuppiramaṇiyam avarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturai eṉṟa nūlaiyum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu appātturaiyam tokukkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. intak kāla varicai aṭippaṭaiyilāṉa nūṟpaṭṭiyal ittokuppil iṇaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. appātturaiyār kuṟittu veḷivantuḷḷa kaṭṭuraikaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷ karuttukkaḷ, avar kuṟitta pāṭalkaḷ tiraṭṭappaṭṭu it tokuppukaḷil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. viṭupaṭṭa nūlkaḷ kiṭaikkac ceytāl aṭutta patippil naṉṟiyuṭaṉ veḷiyiṭappeṟum. avariṉ tirukkuṟaḷ viḷakkavurai ittuṭaṉ iṇaikkavillai. kāraṇam pakkam mikuntu iruppatē. kuṟaikaḷ iruppiṉ, cuṭṭik kāṭṭavum. maṟupatippil avai tiruttikkoḷḷappaṭum.
ittokuppu uruvāvataṟku ellāvakaiyilum muṉṉiṉṟavar aiyā tiru kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār. pakuppu muṟaikaḷaic caripārttuk koṭuttavar aiyā mutumuṉaivar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār. nūlkaḷait taṭṭaccu ceytum, nūliṉ 
uḷ vaṭivamaippiṉaic ceppam ceytum tantavar tirumati. kō. cittirā, tokuppu aṉaittiṟkum ciṟappuṟa mēl aṭṭaikaḷai vaṭivamaittavar celvaṉ. pā. ari (hariṣ), tokuppup paṇiyil tuṇai ceytōr eṉ āyvu māṇavarkaḷ tirumati. pā. mālati, tiru. kā. pāpu, celvaṉ. cu. kōvintarācu, celvi. kā. kayalviḻi. eṉ paṇikaḷ aṉaittiṟkum eṉṟum tuṇainiṟpavar kaṇavar maruttuvar mu. cēkkiḻār. ivarkaḷ aṉaivarukkum eṉṟum eṉ naṉṟiyum aṉpum uriyaṉa. 
kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār

 
nūlāciriyar vivaram
paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyār
iyaṟpeyar		:	nalla civam
piṟappu		:	 24.06.1907 	iṟappu: 26.05.1989
peṟṟōr		:	kācinātap piḷḷai, muttu ilakkumi
piṟanta ūr		:	kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭam, 						āralvāy moḻi (aṟai vāy moḻi)
uṭaṉ piṟantōr		:	taṅkai iruvar, tampiyar iruvar
maṉaiviyar		: 	tirumati. nācciyār, tirumati. alamēlu
vaḷarppu makaḷ		:	tirumati. mallikā
toṭakkak kalvi		:	āralvāy moḻi
paḷḷik kalvi		:	nākarkōvil
kallūrik kalvi		:	tiruvaṉantapuram
		:	iḷaṅkalai āṅkilam (āṉars), 					mutukalai tamiḻ, inti `vicārat’, el.ṭi.
kaṟṟa moḻikaḷ		:	40 (puḻakkattil - tamiḻ, āṅkilam, 					camacukirutam, malaiyāḷam, inti)
nūlkaḷ		:	120 (āṅkilam, 5)
itaḻpaṇi		:	tirāviṭaṉ, jasṭis, intiyā, pāratatēvi, 					ciṉimā ulakam, lōkōpakāri, 						tārus islām, kumaraṉ, teṉṟal, viṭutalai.
paṇi	:	-	1936-37 tirunelvēli nācarēt 				pakutiyil intip piraccāra capā āciriyar.
		-	1937-1939 nellai em.ṭi.ṭi. 				kallūri inti āciriyar.
		-	paḷḷi āciriyar, 						ceṭṭināṭṭil amarāvatiputtūr maṟṟum			kōṉāppaṭṭu.
		-	1947-1949 maiya araciṉ ceytit 				toṭarputuṟaiyil paṇi
		-	1959 - 1965 ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻaka 			āṅkilam tamiḻ akarātip paṇiyil 				iṇai āciriyar.
		-	1975-1979 tamiḻaka varalāṟṟuk kuḻu 				uṟuppiṉar
aṟiñar toṭarpu:
-	toṭakkattil kāntiya cintaṉai.
-	1938-39 il inti etirppu iyakkam, periyār, aṇṇā, pāratitācaṉ kavimaṇi tēciya vināyakam piḷḷai, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, pāvēntar, pāvalarēṟu, tēvanēyap pāvāṇar  maṟṟum camakāla aṟiñar perumakkaḷ, paṭaip pāḷumaikaḷ toṭarpu
virutukaḷ: 	
-	maturaiyil  nikaḻnta 5āvatu ulakat tamiḻ mānāṭṭil poṟkiḻiyum kēṭayamum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu, 
- 	1973 il centamiḻc celvar, cēlam tamiḻakap pulavar kuḻu kūṭṭattil `cāṉṟōr paṭṭam’, `tamiḻaṉpar’ paṭṭam.
-	1981 caṉavari 26 il tamiḻnāṭu iyal, icai, nāṭaka maṉṟam cārpil `kalaimāmaṇi’.
-	1983 il tamiḻnāṭu aracu vaḻaṅkiya `tiru.vi.ka.’ virutu, taṅkap patakkam.
-	maturaik kāmarācar palkalaik kaḻakam ciṟappittu vaḻaṅkiya `pēravaic cemmal’ virutu.
-	1961 il ceṉṉait tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar caṅkattiṉ talaivar.
-	1970 il pārīcil naṭaipeṟṟa ulakat tamiḻ mānāṭṭil ciṟappu uṟuppiṉarākak kalantu koṇṭār.
-	iṅkilāntu ākcupōṭu palkalaikkaḻakam ivaratu `teṉṉāṭṭup pōrkkaḷaṅkaḷ’ nūlai aṅkup paṭikkum mēlpaṭṭap paṭippu māṇavarkaḷukkup pāṭamāka vaikkappaṭṭu iruntatu.
paṉmoḻippulavariṉ vāḻkkai varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ:
-	aṟivuc curaṅkam kā. appātturaiyār, mukamāmaṇi, māṇavar patippakam, ceṉṉai -17, 2005.
-	paṉmoḻip pulavar kā. appātturaiyār,		pērā.muṉaivar. ku.ve. pālacuppiramaṇiyam, cākittiya akātemi, 2007.

 
patippāḷar vivaram
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ 
piṟanta nāḷ		:	3.7.1948
piṟanta ūr		:	uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭu añcal						urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625						tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi		:	kallūri pukumuka vakuppu
ippōtaiya toḻil	:	puḻakkattil illāta paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷait 				tēṭiyeṭuttu veḷiyiṭal
āṟṟiyuḷḷa potuppaṇikaḷ
1965il paḷḷi māṇavaṉāka iruntapōtē moḻip pōrāṭṭattil muṉaippākap paṅkēṟṟut taḷaippaṭuttap peṟṟu 48 nāḷkaḷ ciṟaiyil iruntavar.
piṟanta ūrākiya uṟantairāyaṉkuṭikkāṭṭil `ūrnalaṉ vaḷarccik kaḻakam’ eṉum camūka amaippil iruntu ūr nalappaṇi āṟṟiyavar.
urattanāṭṭil `tamiḻar urimaik kaḻakam’ eṉṉum amaippaiyum, pāvāṇar paṭippakattaiyum naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ iṇaintu niṟuvit tamiḻmoḻi, tamiḻiṉa, tamiḻaka mēmpāṭṭiṟku uḻaittavar. iḷam talaimuṟaikkut tamiḻt toṇṭāṟṟiyavar.
pēraṟiñar aṇṇāviṉ matuvilakkuk koḷkaiyai neñcil ēnti urattanāṭu matuvilakkuk kuḻuviṉ mukkiya amaippāḷarkaḷil oruvarāka iruntu ceyalāṟṟiyavar. 1975-il tamiḻnāṭu caṭṭamaṉṟattil `urattanāṭu tiṭṭam’ eṉṟu pārāṭṭap peṟṟa matuvilakkut tiṭṭam veṟṟi peṟa uḻaittavar.
tamiḻmaṇ patippakattai niṟuvi puḻakkattil illāta  paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷaiyum, putiya paṭaippu ilakkiyaṅkaḷaiyum, 
19  ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇṭut toṭakkat tilum vāḻnta tamiḻccāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻuti vaittuc ceṉṟa tamiḻiṉ aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷait tēṭi eṭuttu muḻumaiyākap poruḷ vaḻip pirittu, kāla niralil toṭar toṭarāka  veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉi muttirai patittu varupavar.
potunilai
taṉittamiḻ iyakkat tantai maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ, tantai periyār, pāvēntar pāratitācaṉ, pēraṟiñar aṇṇā,  moḻinūl mūtaṟiñar ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar,  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār ākiyōrai vaḻikāṭṭikaḷākak koṇṭu avartam koḷkai kaḷai niṟaivēṟṟa ayarātu uḻaittu varupavar.
 
tokuppāciriyar vivaram
muṉaivar kalpaṉā cēkkiḻār
piṟanta nāḷ		:	5.6.1972
piṟanta ūr		:	okkanāṭu kīḻaiyūr							urattanāṭu vaṭṭam - 614 625						tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam.
kalvi		:	mutukalai (tamiḻ, moḻiyiyal, kaṇiṉiyiyal) 				muṉaivar
ippōtaiya paṇi	:	utavip pērāciriyar, tamiḻiyiyal tuṟai,				aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakam.
āṟṟiyuḷḷa kalvippaṇikaḷ
-	aṇṇāmalaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻiyal tuṟaiyil 12 āṇṭukaḷ utavip pērāciriyar paṇi.
-	tirukkuṟaḷ paritiyār uraip patippu, pariti urai āyvu.
- 	puṟanāṉūṟṟil tamiḻar vāḻviyal, aiṅkuṟunūṟṟu urupaṉiyal pakuppāyvu, caṅka ilakkiyap peṇpāṟ pulavarkaḷ pāṭal kaḷ moḻinaṭai - matippīṭu (tokuppu), caṅka ilakkiya ūrppeyar āyvukaḷ ākiya nūlkaḷiṉ āciriyar.
- 	palkalaikkaḻaka māṉiyakkuḻu, cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉattiṉ mūlam āyvuttiṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟu āyvukaḷai nikaḻttiyuḷḷār.
-	palkalaikkaḻaka māṉiyakkuḻuviṉāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷa mēlāyviṉai (ñaṉuku) mēṟkoṇṭu varukiṟār.
-	50kkum mēṟpaṭṭa āyvuk kaṭṭuraikaḷai eḻutiyuḷḷār.
-	malēciyāvil nikaḻnta taṉittamiḻ iyakka nūṟṟāṇṭu viḻāvil ciṟappu aḻaippāḷarāka aḻaikkappaṭṭār.
-	ilaṅkaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa urainaṭai mānāṭṭil kalantu koṇṭu kaṭṭurai vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār.
- 	cemmoḻit tamiḻāyvu mattiya niṟuvaṉattāl vaḻaṅkap paṭṭa kuṭiyaracu talaivariṉ iḷam aṟiñar virutiṉaip peṟṟuḷḷār.
